title: Explicit model setup repair and real-model recovery
tool: native candidate checks and bounded process monitor
---

The Mac runtime exposes explicit repair only when an owned, regular, unshared activation record cannot be read. It preserves the original record by an exclusive rename under its owner lease, records only new intent and requires complete weight verification plus a fresh health check before activation. Valid history, symlinks, shared inodes, a concurrently repaired record, a loaded owner and active work refuse this operation. Retry does not silently archive damaged history.

The complete Mac development suite passes, including interruption before/after archival, byte preservation for empty, malformed and oversized records, queue resumption through ordinary model admission, saved settings/conversation preservation and native Light, Dark and System settings checks. Its single-worker process tree peaks at 1,843,318,624 bytes inside the frozen six-GB envelope. The three repair-screen appearances were also visually inspected; the screenshot digests and raw UI outputs are retained below.

The actual source-frozen Mac check executable then passes the expanded real-model activation sequence. After a normal activation, a disposable test record is damaged, load refuses before allocation, explicit repair preserves those exact bytes, and a freshly authenticated healthy load completes its response with the same saved settings and new generation. The one model process peaks at 5,776,773,872 physical bytes inside its ten-GB ceiling. Installed weights and ordinary user activation records are untouched. This is functional recovery evidence for the supported original pack, not an alternate-pack qualification, installed app or public release.

The Mac suite source snapshot binds its actual executable and includes the check sources; its inherited wrapper's unused engine-monitor hash is not the Mac executable identity. Subsequent engine workspace edits do not alter these frozen artifacts.

Local home prefixes are replaced with <HOME>. Original lengths and SHA-256 identify the unmodified files. Large or whitespace-bearing normalized UTF-8 transcripts are losslessly zlib-compressed and base64 encoded. Decode with `zlib.decompress(base64.b64decode(block))` and check the normalized digest. Every encoded block is verified before writing. Frozen binaries, source archives and weights remain in bounded local staging; the receipts bind their exact bytes.

### .build/quantization-research/freeze-activation-repair-v1.py

Original bytes: 2222. SHA-256: `1e07cc6f5f49aa7faa468f09cea50f3b47b957bbc2df08c96aa9e24e9193da2a`.

Normalized bytes: 2222. SHA-256: `1e07cc6f5f49aa7faa468f09cea50f3b47b957bbc2df08c96aa9e24e9193da2a`.

````text
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,shutil,subprocess,tarfile
r=Path('.build/quantization-research');run=r/'activation-repair-mac-v1';out=r/'frozen-activation-repair-v1'
assert json.loads((run/'receipt.json').read_text())['complete']
before=(run/'inputs-before.json').read_bytes();after=(run/'inputs-after.json').read_bytes();assert before==after
app=json.loads(before);sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
for name,digest in app['files'].items(): assert sha(name)==digest,name
paths={Path(p) for p in app['files'] if not p.endswith('.metallib')}
for base in ['apps/macos/Checks','apps/macos/NativeChecks','apps/macos/CLI']:
 paths.update(p for p in Path(base).rglob('*') if p.is_file())
paths.update(Path(p) for p in ['Tools/check_sevra_mac.sh','Tools/check_sevra_memory_ui.sh','Tools/check_sevra_apps_ui.sh','Tools/check_sevra_thinking_ui.sh'])
allocated=sum(p.stat().st_blocks*512 for p in r.rglob('*') if p.is_file())
assert allocated+300_000_000<370_000_000_000
out.mkdir()
binpath=Path(subprocess.check_output(['swift','build','--package-path','apps/macos','-c','release','--show-bin-path'],text=True).strip())
shutil.copy2(binpath/'sevra-mac-checks',out/'sevra-mac-checks')
shutil.copy2('Tools/lib/mlx-0.32.2.metallib',out/'mlx.metallib')
with tarfile.open(out/'source.tar.gz','w:gz') as tar:
 for p in sorted(paths): tar.add(p,arcname=str(p),recursive=False)
identity={'schema':1,'scope':'Local development check binary, not a signed or published release. App source inputs match before and after the monitored full Mac suite. Check, native-check and CLI sources were captured afterward while unchanged. The wrapper engine binary hash is not the app executable; this receipt binds the actual check executable.', 'build_receipt_sha256':sha(run/'receipt.json'),'app_inputs':app,'source_files':{str(p):sha(p) for p in sorted(paths)},'source_archive_sha256':sha(out/'source.tar.gz'),'binary_sha256':sha(out/'sevra-mac-checks'),'metallib_sha256':sha(out/'mlx.metallib'),'research_allocated_bytes_before':allocated}
(out/'build-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in identity.items() if k not in ('app_inputs','source_files')}))
````

### .build/quantization-research/run-activation-repair-real-v1.py

Original bytes: 3391. SHA-256: `c6405065d67d8ca9326b201bd8caf531a06643e763473edc3fedc85f3fd27bb9`.

Normalized bytes: 3391. SHA-256: `c6405065d67d8ca9326b201bd8caf531a06643e763473edc3fedc85f3fd27bb9`.

````text
from pathlib import Path
import ctypes,ctypes.util,hashlib,json,os,subprocess,sys,time
sys.path.insert(0,'Tools')
from context_qualification import quiet_preflight
from prefill_bench import terminate_child_tree,vm_snapshot
r=Path('.build/quantization-research').absolute();out=r/'activation-repair-real-v1';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
cli=r/'frozen-activation-repair-v1/sevra-mac-checks';binary=sha(cli)
assert json.loads((cli.parent/'build-identity.json').read_text())['binary_sha256']==binary
record={'schema':1,'complete':False,'qualification':False,'scope':'Functional actual-model activation and explicit damaged-record repair. One model process, no timing qualification.','binary_sha256':binary,'metallib_sha256':sha(cli.parent/'mlx.metallib'),'build_identity_sha256':sha(cli.parent/'build-identity.json'),'resource_protocol_sha256':sha(r/'activation-repair-resource-v1.json'),'runs':[]}
save=lambda:(out/'receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
env=dict(os.environ)
for key in list(env):
 if key.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG','VQ_','VQLAB_')):del env[key]
env['SEVRA_DBMD']=str(Path.home()/'.dbmd/bin/dbmd')
commands=[
 ('activation',[str(cli),'--activation-real','--home',str(out/'activation-history')],10),
]
record['commands']=commands;save()
lib=ctypes.CDLL(ctypes.util.find_library('proc'));child=None;start=time.monotonic()
try:
 for name,command,limit in commands:
  before=quiet_preflight(limit+3);row={'case':name,'command':command,'before':before,'maximum_process_bytes':int(limit*1e9),'peak_tree_bytes':0,'samples':0};record['runs'].append(row);save();started=time.monotonic()
  with (out/(name+'.log')).open('w') as log:
   child=subprocess.Popen(command,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
   while child.poll() is None:
    rows=[tuple(map(int,line.split())) for line in subprocess.check_output(['ps','-axo','pid=,ppid='],text=True,timeout=5).splitlines()];pids={child.pid}
    while True:
     more=pids|{pid for pid,parent in rows if parent in pids}
     if more==pids:break
     pids=more
    total=0
    for pid in pids:
     b=ctypes.create_string_buffer(296)
     if lib.proc_pid_rusage(pid,4,b)==0:total+=max(int.from_bytes(b.raw[72:80],'little'),int.from_bytes(b.raw[240:248],'little'))
    row['peak_tree_bytes']=max(row['peak_tree_bytes'],total);row['samples']+=1
    if total>limit*1e9:raise RuntimeError('process envelope exceeded')
    if vm_snapshot()['reclaimable_bytes']<3e9:raise RuntimeError('lost three-GB real headroom')
    if subprocess.check_output(['sysctl','-n','kern.memorystatus_vm_pressure_level'],text=True,timeout=5).strip()!='1':raise RuntimeError('OS memory pressure')
    if time.monotonic()-started>1800 or time.monotonic()-start>1800:raise RuntimeError('time envelope exceeded')
    time.sleep(.25)
   row['exit_code']=child.returncode;row['seconds']=time.monotonic()-started;row['after']=vm_snapshot();save()
   if child.returncode:raise RuntimeError(name+' failed; dependent phases not run')
   child=None

 assert sha(cli)==binary
 record['complete']=True
except BaseException as error:
 record['failure']=type(error).__name__+': '+str(error)
 if child is not None and child.poll() is None:terminate_child_tree(child)
 save();raise
finally:save()
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in record.items() if k != 'runs'}))
````
